- **migration-checklist-generation**: Turns findings and an ordered plan from the other upgrade skills into a plain, human-readable checklist someone can follow by hand, without needing an agent present to execute it. USE FOR: "give me a checklist to migrate this project", "write up the upgrade steps so a teammate can run them", producing a durable, shareable artifact from an already-completed diagnosis. DO NOT USE FOR: detecting drift or producing the plan itself (see the detection skills and fork-upgrade-ordering), or executing the steps (see incremental-template-sync). This skill only formats output. ([ShawnDelaineBellazanLoop/AgentSkills](https://github.com/ShawnDelaineBellazanLoop/AgentSkills/tree/HEAD/plugins/agent-skills-upgrade/skills/migration-checklist-generation))
